here is also the telemetry of the last few runs /using-superpowers /data-analyst 


also here is what claude said based on that telemetry
Three things in this log change my advice, and one of them says some of your recent tuning runs tested nothing.

1. The kp/kd changes had no effect, and the kd_corner runs never entered corner mode.
Every line I checked by hand (about a dozen, across different k_head settings) fits TS = k_cte·F + k_head·H, and in corner mode S = TS + 0.45·FS. No kp or kd term shows up anywhere. So the kp_corner sweep from 2.59 to 4.0 and the kd_* changes didn't alter steering, unless they feed something you don't log. The three kd_corner runs (0.22, 0.2, 0.18) also had FV:0 and FS:0 on every tick. The front camera was invalid for the whole run, so ST was always S and corner gains and corner mode were never active. Check the code to confirm the kp/kd gains are dead.

2. My unit assumption was wrong.
H and FS hit ±500-600 in corners, so they're not degrees. Tenths of a degree is my guess, which would fit the ±650 clamp on S, but check the code. My 15/30 blend range and the 10/25 H_LO/H_HI are off by that factor. The pasted claim that straight weave is "3-7° (H:30-70)" isn't supported either. Your ST:S stretches regularly show H at ±100-300, with the inside wheel dropping to 30-60 on them.

3. The mode flicker is real, and it shows up exactly where we feared.
There are single-tick drops to ST:S mid-corner, and they cost you the feedforward and the speed limit. One example is H:-495 TS:-627 ... FV:0 ST:S VR:85 in the middle of a hairpin, where the outer wheel jumps from 50 to 85. That supports a slow-release blend over a hard flag.

Two further problems for my design:

The flag isn't tied to FS. I see ST:C with FS near 0 and ST:S with FS around -250. If the blend input is filtered_front_angle, it won't match when the flag fires. Use whatever raw value the flag is built from.
The heading hold is unproven. H flips sign through corners and swings ±200 on the exits. A hold based on |H| either drops mid-apex when H crosses zero, or stays high all lap.

Next steps

Rerun the kd_corner comparison with FV:1, or skip it if the code confirms kp/kd are dead.
Log a stretch that is only a straight (about 3 s, ideally 100% speed). I need that peak |H| to set H_LO, and nothing in this log is a clean straight. Paste it and I'll set the numbers.
Build the blend front-camera-only first: fast attack, release over about 25-30 ticks, speed and differential following the blend. If apex washout still shows up after that, add the heading hold.

Also, S sits pinned at -650 for 5-9 ticks in most corners. During those ticks no corner gain change can do anything.
